def generate_0d3d703e() -> dict:
    diff_lb = 0
    print(f"diff_lb: {diff_lb}")
    diff_ub = 1
    print(f"diff_ub: {diff_ub}")
    
    incols = (1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 8, 9)
    outcols = (5, 6, 4, 3, 1, 2, 9, 8)
    k = len(incols)
    print(f"k: {k}")
    
    h = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (4, 30))
    print(f"h: {h}")
    
    w = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (4, 30))
    print(f"w: {w}")
    
    gi = canvas(-1, (h, w))
    print(f"gi: {gi}")
    
    inds = asindices(gi)
    print(f"inds: {inds}")
    
    numc = unifint(diff_lb, diff_ub, (1, k))
    print(f"numc: {numc}")
    
    idxes = sample(interval(0, k, 1), numc)
    print(f"idxes: {idxes}")
    
    for ij in inds:
        idx = choice(idxes)
        print(f"idx: {idx}")
        gi = fill(gi, incols[idx], {ij})
        print(f"gi after fill: {gi}")
    
    return {'input': gi}
